Patenting means disclosing the invention publicly, so a process that can be kept hidden is often better as a trade secret — the Coca-Cola formula would have entered the public domain a century ago had it been patented. The rights are also territorial: protection must be obtained country by country, with treaties easing the process rather than creating a single global right.'],ex:['Software may be simultaneously copyrighted as code, patented as a method, sold under a trademark and built on trade secrets.','A recipe cannot generally be copyrighted, since a list of ingredients is not creative expression — hence the secrecy.'],facts:['Copyright requires no registration in most countries, though registration helps enormously when enforcing it.','A trademark can be lost by becoming generic, which is why brand owners police the use of names like escalator and aspirin — both already lost.'],keys:[['Copyright','Automatic protection of expression, not of ideas.'],['Patent','Time-limited monopoly in exchange for full disclosure.'],['Trade secret','Indefinite protection that ends the moment it is public.']],sum:'Expression, inventions, identifiers and secrets are four distinct rights with different durations and different tradeoffs.'},

{t:'Why time feels faster as we get older',c:'Psychology',m:11,hook:'A year at fifty is a fiftieth of your life. At ten it was a tenth. That alone explains a lot.',why:'It is a universal experience, it has real explanations, and one of them suggests what to do about it.',body:['Two accounts do most of the work. The proportional theory says each interval is judged against the total you have lived, so a year occupies a steadily shrinking fraction of your experience and feels correspondingly shorter. It is intuitive and probably part of the story, though not a complete one.','The stronger explanation is about memory density. Your sense of how long a period was, looking back, depends on how many distinct memories it contains. Novelty produces detailed encoding; routine produces almost none, because the brain does not store what it can predict. Childhood is dense with first experiences and therefore feels enormous in retrospect. Adult life becomes routinised — same commute, same work, same weeks — and those years leave few markers, so they compress. This gives a genuine prescription: novelty, travel, learning and changes of environment measurably increase the memories a period leaves behind, and lengthen it in hindsight.'],ex:['A week of travel feels longer in memory than a month of ordinary routine, despite being four times shorter.','The reminiscence bump means people recall disproportionately many memories from adolescence and early adulthood, exactly the densest period of firsts.'],facts:['Prospective and retrospective timing differ: a boring hour drags while it happens and vanishes in memory, and an absorbing one does the reverse.','Attention to time itself slows it — watching the clock lengthens the experienced duration but leaves no more memory behind.'],keys:[['Proportional theory','Each year measured against a longer life.'],['Memory density','Duration in hindsight judged by how many memories a period left.'],['Novelty encoding','New experience being stored in detail, routine barely at all.']],sum:'Routine years leave few memories and collapse in hindsight, which is why novelty is what makes time feel long.'},

"use strict";
/* ============================================================================
   APP — views, the scrolling topic stage, search, filters and progress.
   ========================================================================== */
const $ = id => document.getElementById(id);
const CATS = [...new Set(TOPICS.map(t => t.c))].sort();

/* ---- progress, stored in this browser only ----------------------------- */
const KEY = 'lsn.progress.v1';
const Progress = {
  set: new Set(),
  load(){
    try{
      const raw = localStorage.getItem(KEY);
      if (raw) this.set = new Set(JSON.parse(raw) || []);
    }catch(e){ /* a corrupt or blocked store must never stop the site loading */ }
    // Drop entries for topics that no longer exist, so the count cannot exceed
    // the library after topics are edited.
    const titles = new Set(TOPICS.map(t => t.t));
    for (const t of [...this.set]) if (!titles.has(t)) this.set.delete(t);
  },
  save(){ try{ localStorage.setItem(KEY, JSON.stringify([...this.set])); }catch(e){} },
  has(t){ return this.set.has(t.t); },
  toggle(t){ this.set.has(t.t) ? this.set.delete(t.t) : this.set.add(t.t); this.save(); paintProgress(); },
  count(){ return this.set.size; }
};
Progress.load();

function paintProgress(){
  const n = Progress.count(), total = TOPICS.length;
  $('pcount').textContent = n + ' / ' + total;
  $('pbar').style.width = (total ? n / total * 100 : 0) + '%';
  if ($('stats').children.length) renderStats();
  // Any visible card or topic may now be stale.
  if (state.view === 'explore') renderGrid();
  if (state.view === 'topic' && state.open) renderArticle(state.open);
  paintStageMeta();
}

/* ---- theme -------------------------------------------------------------- */
const THEME_KEY = 'lsn.theme';
function applyTheme(mode){
  document.documentElement.dataset.theme = mode;
  // A filled sun for light, a crescent for dark, so the button shows the state.
  $('themeIcon').setAttribute('d', mode === 'dark'
    ? 'M12 3v2M12 19v2M5 12H3M21 12h-2M6 6l1.5 1.5M16.5 16.5L18 18M18 6l-1.5 1.5M7.5 16.5L6 18M12 8a4 4 0 1 1 0 8 4 4 0 0 1 0-8z'
    : 'M21 13a8.5 8.5 0 1 1-10-10 7 7 0 0 0 10 10z');
  try{ localStorage.setItem(THEME_KEY, mode); }catch(e){}
}
applyTheme((()=>{ try{ return localStorage.getItem(THEME_KEY) ||
  (matchMedia('(prefers-color-scheme: dark)').matches ? 'dark' : 'light'); }catch(e){ return 'light'; } })());
$('themeBtn').onclick = () =>
  applyTheme(document.documentElement.dataset.theme === 'dark' ? 'light' : 'dark');

/* ---- background palette -------------------------------------------------
   Taste is not something screenshots settle, so the colours are switchable
   and the choice is remembered. */
const PALETTES = [
  ['aurora','Aurora','#6D5BFF,#B14BFF,#00C2FF,#FF4BA6'],
  ['sunset','Sunset','#FF6B3D,#FF3D77,#FFAE2E,#C74BFF'],
  ['reef',  'Reef',  '#00D4C8,#0091FF,#3DE68A,#00C2FF'],
  ['ember', 'Ember', '#FF3D3D,#FF7A1A,#FFB800,#FF2D78'],
  ['neon',  'Neon',  '#00FFC2,#00A3FF,#B14BFF,#FF2D78']
];
const PAL_KEY = 'lsn.palette';

function applyPalette(id){
  // 'aurora' is the bare :root block, so it is set by removing the attribute.
  if (id === 'aurora') delete document.documentElement.dataset.pal;
  else document.documentElement.dataset.pal = id;
  try{ localStorage.setItem(PAL_KEY, id); }catch(e){}
  for (const b of $('palPop').querySelectorAll('.swatch'))
    b.classList.toggle('on', b.dataset.pal === id);
}

$('palPop').innerHTML = '<h4>Background</h4>' + PALETTES.map(([id, name, stops]) =>
  `<button class="swatch" data-pal="${id}">
     <i style="background:linear-gradient(90deg,${stops})"></i>${name}</button>`).join('');

$('palPop').querySelectorAll('.swatch').forEach(b => {
  b.onclick = () => { applyPalette(b.dataset.pal); $('palPop').classList.remove('on'); };
});

$('palBtn').onclick = e => { e.stopPropagation(); $('palPop').classList.toggle('on'); };
addEventListener('click', e => {
  if (!e.target.closest('.palwrap')) $('palPop').classList.remove('on');
});

applyPalette((()=>{ try{ return localStorage.getItem(PAL_KEY) || 'aurora'; }catch(e){ return 'aurora'; } })());

/* ---- app state ---------------------------------------------------------- */
const state = { view:'learn', i:0, started:false, filter:'All', open:null, order:[] };

/* A shuffled order so the same topics do not appear in the same sequence every
   visit. Fisher-Yates, seeded by nothing in particular — variety is the point. */
function reshuffle(){
  state.order = TOPICS.map((_, i) => i);
  for (let i = state.order.length - 1; i > 0; i--){
    const j = Math.floor(Math.random() * (i + 1));
    [state.order[i], state.order[j]] = [state.order[j], state.order[i]];
  }
}
reshuffle();
const current = () => TOPICS[state.order[state.i]];

/* ---- views -------------------------------------------------------------- */
function go(view){
  state.view = view;
  for (const v of ['learn','explore','topic','about','gallery'])
    $('v-' + v).classList.toggle('on', v === view);
  for (const b of $('navlinks').children)
    b.classList.toggle('on', b.dataset.go === view);
  if (view === 'explore') renderGrid();
  if (view === 'about') renderStats();
  if (view === 'gallery') renderGallery();
  // Object URLs for clips are only valid while their view is on screen.
  if (view !== 'gallery' && view !== 'topic') dropUrls();
  window.scrollTo({ top:0, behavior:'instant' in window ? 'instant' : 'auto' });
}

for (const b of $('navlinks').children){
  b.onclick = () => {
    const target = b.dataset.go;
    if (target === 'random') return surprise();
    if (target === 'categories'){ go('explore'); document.getElementById('cats').scrollIntoView({behavior:'smooth',block:'center'}); return; }
    go(target);
  };
}

/* ---- the topic stage ---------------------------------------------------- */
function topicMarkup(t){
  const done = Progress.has(t);
  return t.t.replace(/^How /, 'How ') +
    `<div class="meta">
       <span class="chip cat">${t.c}</span>
       <span class="chip">${t.m} min read</span>
       ${done ? '<span class="chip done">✓ Learned</span>' : ''}
     </div>`;
}
function paintStageMeta(){
  if (!state.started) return;
  const el = $('topicEl');
  if (el && el.dataset.title === current().t) el.innerHTML = topicMarkup(current());
}

/* Swap the visible topic. `dir` is +1 for the next topic and -1 for the
   previous one, which decides whether the text slides up or down — the
   direction is what makes the list feel like a list rather than a shuffle. */
let animating = false;
function show(dir){
  const wrap = $('stagewrap');
  const old = $('topicEl');
  const t = current();

  const el = document.createElement('div');
  el.className = 'topic ' + (dir >= 0 ? 'in-up' : 'in-dn');
  el.id = 'topicEl';
  el.dataset.title = t.t;
  el.setAttribute('role','button');
  el.tabIndex = 0;
  el.innerHTML = topicMarkup(t);
  el.onclick = () => openTopic(t);
  el.onkeydown = e => { if (e.key === 'Enter' || e.key === ' ') { e.preventDefault(); openTopic(t); } };

  if (old && old !== el){
    old.id = '';
    old.className = 'topic ' + (dir >= 0 ? 'out-up' : 'out-dn');
    // Remove after the leave animation, not before, or it vanishes instantly.
    setTimeout(() => old.remove(), 430);
  }
  wrap.appendChild(el);

  $('counterline').textContent = (state.i + 1) + ' of ' + TOPICS.length +
    ' · ' + Progress.count() + ' learned';
}

function move(step){
  if (animating || spinning) return;
  animating = true;
  setTimeout(() => { animating = false; }, 260);   // rate-limit, not a lock-out
  state.i = (state.i + step + TOPICS.length) % TOPICS.length;
  show(step);
}

/* ==========================================================================
   THE ROULETTE

   Pressing Start spins a reel of topics past the centre and lands on one.

   The winner is chosen up front and the reel is built so that winner sits at
   a known row; the animation then eases from 0 to that exact distance. Doing
   it the other way round — spinning freely and deciding where to stop — means
   either snapping at the end or landing between two rows, and both are
   immediately visible.

   Tune the feel here:
      SPIN_ROWS   how many topics fly past (the sense of searching hundreds)
      SPIN_MS     total duration, fast start to full stop
      EASE        the deceleration curve; higher power = harder braking
   ========================================================================== */
const SPIN_ROWS = 78;
const SPIN_MS   = 4600;
const EASE      = t => 1 - Math.pow(1 - t, 5.2);   // very fast → … → stop

let spinning = false;

function spin(onDone){
  if (spinning) return;
  spinning = true;

  const wrap = $('stagewrap');
  // A zero height — a hidden tab, a font still loading — would collapse every
  // row and the reel would appear not to move at all.
  const rowH = Math.max(160, wrap.clientHeight);

  // The winner. Chosen from the whole library, never biased toward the rows
  // shown first, and it becomes the current topic so the arrows carry on from
  // wherever the reel stopped.
  const winner = Math.floor(Math.random() * TOPICS.length);
  state.i = state.order.indexOf(winner);

  // Build the reel: SPIN_ROWS random topics, with the winner placed last.
  const rows = [];
  for (let i = 0; i < SPIN_ROWS; i++) rows.push(Math.floor(Math.random() * TOPICS.length));
  rows.push(winner);

  const reel = document.createElement('div');
  reel.className = 'reel';
  reel.innerHTML = rows.map(i =>
    `<div class="row" style="height:${rowH}px">${esc(TOPICS[i].t)}</div>`).join('');

  const mask = document.createElement('div');
  mask.className = 'reelmask';
  mask.appendChild(reel);

  // Take every topic out of the stage rather than setting opacity to 0.
  // Hiding relies on stacking, masks and opacity all behaving; removing does
  // not, so there is no browser on which the old topic can survive the spin.
  wrap.querySelectorAll('.topic').forEach(el => el.remove());
  wrap.appendChild(mask);

  const distance = SPIN_ROWS * rowH;          // stops with the winner centred
  const t0 = performance.now();
  let lastY = 0;

  function frame(now){
    const t = Math.min(1, (now - t0) / SPIN_MS);
    const y = EASE(t) * distance;

    // Motion blur from the actual frame-to-frame speed, which is what turns
    // the fast section into a smear and the last rows into readable text.
    const speed = Math.abs(y - lastY);
    lastY = y;
    const blur = Math.min(14, speed * 0.075);

    reel.style.transform = `translate3d(0,${-y}px,0)`;
    reel.style.filter = blur > 0.4 ? `blur(${blur.toFixed(2)}px)` : '';

    if (t < 1) return requestAnimationFrame(frame);

    mask.remove();
    spinning = false;
    onDone(TOPICS[winner]);
  }
  requestAnimationFrame(frame);
}

/* The landing beat: the chosen topic pops in sharp, a soft glow blooms behind
   it and a few sparks scatter. Brief and quiet — the point is punctuation, not
   fireworks. */
function landOn(){
  show(1);
  const el = $('topicEl');
  el.classList.add('landed');

  const g = $('glow');
  g.classList.remove('on'); void g.offsetWidth; g.classList.add('on');

  const wrap = $('stagewrap');
  for (let i = 0; i < 10; i++){
    const s = document.createElement('i');
    s.className = 'spark';
    const a = (i / 10) * Math.PI * 2 + Math.random();
    const d = 90 + Math.random() * 110;
    s.style.cssText += `left:50%;top:50%;--dx:${Math.cos(a) * d}px;--dy:${Math.sin(a) * d}px`;
    wrap.appendChild(s);
    void s.offsetWidth; s.classList.add('on');
    setTimeout(() => s.remove(), 900);
  }
}

function start(){
  if (spinning) return;

  const btn = $('startBtn');
  btn.disabled = true;                       // no double-spins
  btn.textContent = 'Finding your topic…';
  $('learn').classList.add('spinning');
  $('eyebrow').textContent = 'Searching 115 things you could learn';

  const finish = () => {
    state.started = true;
    $('learn').classList.remove('spinning');
    $('preStart').style.display = 'none';
    $('postStart').style.display = 'flex';
    $('eyebrow').textContent = '';
    $('yourTopic').classList.add('on');
    $('hint').innerHTML = '<kbd>↑</kbd><kbd>↓</kbd> browse · <kbd>Enter</kbd> open · <kbd>R</kbd> spin again · <kbd>/</kbd> search';
    btn.disabled = false;
    btn.textContent = 'Start learning  →';
    landOn();
  };

  // Respect a reduced-motion preference by choosing without the spin, rather
  // than by playing it faster.
  if (matchMedia('(prefers-reduced-motion: reduce)').matches){
    state.i = Math.floor(Math.random() * TOPICS.length);
    return finish();
  }

  spin(finish);
}

$('startBtn').onclick = start;
$('nextBtn').onclick = () => move(1);
$('prevBtn').onclick = () => move(-1);
$('openBtn').onclick = () => openTopic(current());
$('surpriseBtn').onclick = surprise;
$('restartBtn').onclick = () => {
  reshuffle(); state.i = 0; state.started = false;
  $('preStart').style.display = 'flex';
  $('postStart').style.display = 'none';
  $('yourTopic').classList.remove('on');
  $('eyebrow').textContent = 'Thousands of things you could learn';
  $('hint').textContent = '';
  $('counterline').textContent = '';
  let el = $('topicEl');
  if (!el){
    el = document.createElement('div');
    el.id = 'topicEl'; el.className = 'topic'; el.tabIndex = 0;
    $('stagewrap').appendChild(el);
  }
  el.classList.remove('landed');
  el.style.opacity = '';
  el.innerHTML = idleMarkup();
  el.onclick = () => { if (!state.started) start(); else openTopic(current()); };
};

/* Spin again rather than jumping straight to a topic — the wheel is the point. */
$('surpriseBtn').onclick = () => { if (state.started) respin(); else start(); };
function respin(){
  $('yourTopic').classList.remove('on');
  const el = $('topicEl'); if (el) el.classList.remove('landed');
  if (matchMedia('(prefers-reduced-motion: reduce)').matches){
    state.i = Math.floor(Math.random() * TOPICS.length);
    return landOn();
  }
  spin(() => { $('yourTopic').classList.add('on'); landOn(); });
}

function idleMarkup(){ return TOPICS[state.order[0]].t; }

function surprise(){
  // From anywhere else, go to the homepage and spin — the roulette is the
  // signature interaction, so "random" should show it rather than skip it.
  if (state.view !== 'learn') go('learn');
  if (!state.started) return start();
  respin();
}

/* Manual scrolling through topics, on wheel and on touch. The homepage does not
   scroll the document, so a wheel gesture means "next topic". */
let wheelLock = 0;
$('v-learn').addEventListener('wheel', e => {
  if (!state.started || state.view !== 'learn') return;
  if (Math.abs(e.deltaY) < 8) return;
  const now = Date.now();
  if (now < wheelLock) return;
  wheelLock = now + 320;             // one topic per gesture, not per event
  e.preventDefault();
  move(e.deltaY > 0 ? 1 : -1);
}, { passive:false });

let touchY = null;
$('v-learn').addEventListener('touchstart', e => { touchY = e.touches[0].clientY; }, { passive:true });
$('v-learn').addEventListener('touchend', e => {
  if (touchY === null || !state.started) return;
  const dy = e.changedTouches[0].clientY - touchY;
  touchY = null;
  if (Math.abs(dy) > 48) move(dy < 0 ? 1 : -1);
}, { passive:true });

/* ---- topic page --------------------------------------------------------- */
function openTopic(t){
  state.open = t;
  renderArticle(t);
  go('topic');
}
function esc(s){ return String(s).replace(/[&<>]/g, c => ({'&':'&amp;','<':'&lt;','>':'&gt;'}[c])); }

function renderArticle(t){
  const done = Progress.has(t);
  const related = TOPICS.filter(x => x.c === t.c && x.t !== t.t).slice(0, 3);

  $('article').innerHTML = `
    <div class="tags">
      <span class="chip cat">${esc(t.c)}</span>
      <span class="chip">Estimated learning time: ${t.m} minutes</span>
      ${done ? '<span class="chip done">✓ Learned</span>' : ''}
    </div>
    <h1>${esc(t.t)}</h1>
    <p class="lede">${esc(t.hook)}</p>

    <div class="sec"><h2>Why it matters</h2><div class="callout">${esc(t.why)}</div></div>

    <div class="sec"><h2>The explanation</h2>
      ${t.body.map(p => `<p>${esc(p)}</p>`).join('')}</div>

    <div class="sec"><h2>Examples</h2>
      <ul class="plain">${t.ex.map(e => `<li>${esc(e)}</li>`).join('')}</ul></div>

    <div class="sec"><h2>Worth knowing</h2>
      <ul class="plain">${t.facts.map(f => `<li>${esc(f)}</li>`).join('')}</ul></div>

    <div class="sec"><h2>Key concepts</h2>
      <div class="keys">${t.keys.map(k =>
        `<div><b>${esc(k[0])}</b><span>${esc(k[1])}</span></div>`).join('')}</div></div>

    <div class="summary"><b>In one line</b>${esc(t.sum)}</div>

    <div class="pagefoot">
      <button class="btn ${done ? 'ghost' : 'accent'}" id="markBtn">
        ${done ? '✓ Learned — undo' : 'Mark as learned'}</button>
      <button class="btn ghost" id="nextTopicBtn">Next topic →</button>
      <button class="btn ghost" id="surprise2">Surprise me</button>
    </div>

    <div class="sec"><h2>Explain it back</h2>
      <p style="color:var(--ink-2);margin:0 0 14px">The test of understanding is whether you
         can say it without the page in front of you. Record yourself doing it — the clip is
         saved in this browser, in your gallery.</p>
      <button class="btn ${done ? 'accent' : 'ghost'}" id="recBtn">
        ${done ? '🎬 Record what you learned' : 'Record what you learned'}</button></div>

    <div class="sec" style="display:none"><h2>Your explanations</h2>
      <div class="mine" id="mine"></div></div>

    ${related.length ? `<div class="sec"><h2>More in ${esc(t.c)}</h2>
      <div class="grid" style="padding-bottom:0">${related.map(r => cardMarkup(r)).join('')}</div></div>` : ''}
  `;

  $('markBtn').onclick = () => Progress.toggle(t);
  $('nextTopicBtn').onclick = () => {
    const at = TOPICS.indexOf(t);
    openTopic(TOPICS[(at + 1) % TOPICS.length]);
  };
  $('surprise2').onclick = () => openTopic(TOPICS[Math.floor(Math.random() * TOPICS.length)]);
  $('recBtn').onclick = () => Studio.open(t);
  wireCards($('article'));
  fillMine(t);
}

$('backBtn').onclick = () => go(state.started ? 'learn' : 'explore');

/* ---- explore ------------------------------------------------------------ */
function cardMarkup(t){
  const done = Progress.has(t);
  return `<button class="card" data-topic="${esc(t.t)}">
    <h3>${esc(t.t)}</h3>
    <p class="hook">${esc(t.hook)}</p>
    <div class="foot"><span class="chip cat">${esc(t.c)}</span>
      <span>${t.m} min</span>${done ? '<span class="tick">✓</span>' : ''}</div>
  </button>`;
}
function wireCards(root){
  root.querySelectorAll('.card').forEach(c => {
    c.onclick = () => {
      const t = TOPICS.find(x => x.t === c.dataset.topic);
      if (t) openTopic(t);
    };
  });
}
function renderCats(){
  const counts = {}; TOPICS.forEach(t => counts[t.c] = (counts[t.c] || 0) + 1);
  $('cats').innerHTML = ['All', ...CATS].map(c =>
    `<button class="cat ${c === state.filter ? 'on' : ''}" data-cat="${esc(c)}">${esc(c)}
      <span style="opacity:.5">${c === 'All' ? TOPICS.length : counts[c]}</span></button>`).join('');
  $('cats').querySelectorAll('.cat').forEach(b => {
    b.onclick = () => { state.filter = b.dataset.cat; renderCats(); renderGrid(); };
  });
}
function renderGrid(){
  const list = state.filter === 'All' ? TOPICS : TOPICS.filter(t => t.c === state.filter);
  $('grid').innerHTML = list.length
    ? list.map(cardMarkup).join('')
    : '<div class="empty">Nothing here yet.</div>';
  wireCards($('grid'));
}
renderCats();

/* ---- about stats -------------------------------------------------------- */
function renderStats(){
  const mins = TOPICS.reduce((a, t) => a + t.m, 0);
  $('stats').innerHTML = `
    <div class="stat"><b>${TOPICS.length}</b><span>topics</span></div>
    <div class="stat"><b>${CATS.length}</b><span>categories</span></div>
    <div class="stat"><b>${Progress.count()}</b><span>learned by you</span></div>
    <div class="stat"><b>${Math.round(mins / 60)}h</b><span>of reading in total</span></div>`;
}

/* ---- search ------------------------------------------------------------- */
let selIndex = 0, hits = [];
function openSearch(){
  $('veil').classList.add('on');
  $('q').value = ''; $('q').focus();
  runSearch('');
}
function closeSearch(){ $('veil').classList.remove('on'); }

function runSearch(term){
  const q = term.trim().toLowerCase();
  // Rank title matches above category and hook matches, so typing a subject
  // still finds the topic named after it first.
  hits = (q ? TOPICS.map(t => {
      const title = t.t.toLowerCase(), cat = t.c.toLowerCase(), hook = t.hook.toLowerCase();
      let score = 0;
      if (title.includes(q)) score = title.startsWith(q) ? 100 : 60;
      else if (cat.includes(q)) score = 30;
      else if (hook.includes(q)) score = 15;
      return { t, score };
    }).filter(r => r.score > 0).sort((a, b) => b.score - a.score).map(r => r.t)
    : TOPICS.slice(0, 8));

  selIndex = 0;
  $('results').innerHTML = hits.length
    ? hits.slice(0, 40).map((t, i) =>
        `<button class="result ${i === 0 ? 'sel' : ''}" data-i="${i}">
           <b>${esc(t.t)}</b><small>${esc(t.c)} · ${t.m} min</small></button>`).join('')
    : '<div class="empty" style="padding:34px">No topic matches that.</div>';

  $('results').querySelectorAll('.result').forEach(r => {
    r.onclick = () => { closeSearch(); openTopic(hits[+r.dataset.i]); };
  });
}
function moveSel(d){
  const rows = $('results').querySelectorAll('.result');
  if (!rows.length) return;
  rows[selIndex]?.classList.remove('sel');
  selIndex = (selIndex + d + rows.length) % rows.length;
  rows[selIndex].classList.add('sel');
  rows[selIndex].scrollIntoView({ block:'nearest' });
}
$('searchBtn').onclick = openSearch;
$('q').oninput = e => runSearch(e.target.value);
$('veil').onclick = e => { if (e.target === $('veil')) closeSearch(); };

/* ---- keyboard ----------------------------------------------------------- */
addEventListener('keydown', e => {
  if ($('studio').classList.contains('on')){
    if (e.key === 'Escape') Studio.close();
    return;                       // the studio owns the keyboard while it is open
  }
  const searching = $('veil').classList.contains('on');

  if (searching){
    if (e.key === 'Escape') return closeSearch();
    if (e.key === 'ArrowDown'){ e.preventDefault(); return moveSel(1); }
    if (e.key === 'ArrowUp'){ e.preventDefault(); return moveSel(-1); }
    if (e.key === 'Enter' && hits.length){ closeSearch(); openTopic(hits[selIndex]); }
    return;
  }

  // Never hijack typing.
  if (e.target.tagName === 'INPUT') return;

  if (e.key === '/'){ e.preventDefault(); return openSearch(); }
  if (e.key === 'Escape' && state.view === 'topic') return go(state.started ? 'learn' : 'explore');
  if (e.key.toLowerCase() === 'r'){ e.preventDefault(); return surprise(); }

  if (state.view !== 'learn') return;
  if (e.key === 'ArrowDown'){ e.preventDefault(); state.started ? move(1) : start(); }
  if (e.key === 'ArrowUp'){ e.preventDefault(); if (state.started) move(-1); }
  if (e.key === 'Enter'){ e.preventDefault(); state.started ? openTopic(current()) : start(); }
});


/* =========================================================================
   VIDEO VAULT
   Clips are video blobs, which are far too big for localStorage, so they live
   in IndexedDB. Every call resolves rather than throws: a browser in private
   mode with storage blocked must degrade to "you cannot save clips" and leave
   the rest of the site working.
   ========================================================================= */
const Vault = {
  db:null, broken:false,
  open(){
    if (this.db) return Promise.resolve(this.db);
    if (this.broken || !window.indexedDB) return Promise.resolve(null);
    return new Promise(resolve => {
      let req;
      try{ req = indexedDB.open('lsn.videos', 1); }
      catch(e){ this.broken = true; return resolve(null); }

      req.onupgradeneeded = () => {
        const db = req.result;
        if (!db.objectStoreNames.contains('clips'))
          db.createObjectStore('clips', { keyPath:'id', autoIncrement:true });
      };
      req.onsuccess = () => { this.db = req.result; resolve(this.db); };
      req.onerror   = () => { this.broken = true; resolve(null); };
      req.onblocked = () => resolve(null);
    });
  },
  async tx(mode){
    const db = await this.open();
    return db ? db.transaction('clips', mode).objectStore('clips') : null;
  },
  async add(rec){
    const store = await this.tx('readwrite');
    if (!store) return null;
    return new Promise(resolve => {
      const r = store.add(rec);
      r.onsuccess = () => resolve(r.result);
      r.onerror   = () => resolve(null);
    });
  },
  async all(){
    const store = await this.tx('readonly');
    if (!store) return [];
    return new Promise(resolve => {
      const r = store.getAll();
      // Newest first: the clip you just made should be the one you see.
      r.onsuccess = () => resolve((r.result || []).sort((a, b) => b.at - a.at));
      r.onerror   = () => resolve([]);
    });
  },
  async del(id){
    const store = await this.tx('readwrite');
    if (!store) return;
    return new Promise(resolve => { const r = store.delete(id); r.onsuccess = r.onerror = () => resolve(); });
  }
};

/* ---- small helpers ------------------------------------------------------ */
function clock(sec){
  sec = Math.max(0, Math.round(sec));
  return Math.floor(sec / 60) + ':' + String(sec % 60).padStart(2, '0');
}
function weight(bytes){
  if (bytes < 1024 * 1024) return Math.max(1, Math.round(bytes / 1024)) + ' KB';
  return (bytes / 1048576).toFixed(bytes > 10485760 ? 0 : 1) + ' MB';
}
function when(ms){
  const d = new Date(ms), now = Date.now();
  if (now - ms < 86400000 && d.getDate() === new Date().getDate())
    return 'Today, ' + d.toLocaleTimeString([], { hour:'numeric', minute:'2-digit' });
  return d.toLocaleDateString([], { day:'numeric', month:'short', year:'numeric' });
}
let toastTimer = 0;
function toast(msg){
  let el = $('toast');
  if (!el){
    el = document.createElement('div');
    el.id = 'toast'; el.className = 'toast';
    document.body.appendChild(el);
  }
  el.textContent = msg;
  el.classList.add('on');
  clearTimeout(toastTimer);
  toastTimer = setTimeout(() => el.classList.remove('on'), 2600);
}

/* =========================================================================
   THE STUDIO — camera, recorder, and the save panel.
   ========================================================================= */
const Studio = {
  stream:null, rec:null, chunks:[], blob:null, url:null,
  mode:'off',            // off | live | rec | review
  t0:0, tick:0, topic:null, counting:false,

  MAX_MS: 10 * 60 * 1000,   // a hard ceiling, or one forgotten tab fills the disk

  /* The first mime type the browser will actually record in. Chrome and
     Firefox want webm, Safari only ever offers mp4, so asking is the only
     portable way to find out. */
  mime(){
    const wanted = ['video/webm;codecs=vp9,opus','video/webm;codecs=vp8,opus',
                    'video/webm','video/mp4;codecs=h264,aac','video/mp4'];
    for (const m of wanted)
      if (window.MediaRecorder && MediaRecorder.isTypeSupported(m)) return m;
    return '';
  },

  open(topic){
    this.topic = topic || null;
    $('studio').classList.add('on');
    $('stTitle').textContent = topic ? 'Explain it in your own words' : 'Record an explanation';
    $('stSub').textContent = topic
      ? '“' + topic.t + '” — say it as if to someone who has never heard of it. Where you stumble is the part you have not learned yet.'
      : 'Pick the topic below when you are done. Say it as if to someone who has never heard of it.';
    this.reset();
    this.fillTopics();
    document.body.style.overflow = 'hidden';
  },

  close(){
    this.stopStream();
    $('studio').classList.remove('on');
    document.body.style.overflow = '';
    this.reset();
  },

  reset(){
    this.chunks = []; this.blob = null;
    if (this.url){ URL.revokeObjectURL(this.url); this.url = null; }
    clearInterval(this.tick);

    const v = $('stVideo');
    v.srcObject = null; v.removeAttribute('src'); v.controls = false; v.muted = true;
    v.classList.add('mirror');

    $('stMsg').style.display = '';
    $('stRec').classList.remove('on');
    $('stDot').classList.remove('live');
    $('stDot').disabled = false;
    $('stSave').style.display = 'none';
    $('stTime').textContent = '0:00';
    $('stHint').textContent = 'Press the red button to turn on your camera.';
    this.mode = 'off';
    this.counting = false;
  },

  stopStream(){
    if (this.rec && this.rec.state !== 'inactive'){ try{ this.rec.stop(); }catch(e){} }
    this.rec = null;
    if (this.stream){ this.stream.getTracks().forEach(t => t.stop()); this.stream = null; }
    clearInterval(this.tick);
  },

  message(title, body){
    $('stMsg').style.display = '';
    $('stMsg').innerHTML = '<b>' + esc(title) + '</b>' + (body ? '<span>' + esc(body) + '</span>' : '');
  },

  async camera(){
    if (!navigator.mediaDevices || !navigator.mediaDevices.getUserMedia){
      this.message('No camera available here',
        'This browser will not hand over a camera on a local file. You can still upload a video you recorded elsewhere.');
      $('stHint').textContent = 'Use “Upload a video instead”.';
      return false;
    }
    this.message('Asking for your camera…', 'Allow access in the prompt at the top of the window.');
    try{
      this.stream = await navigator.mediaDevices.getUserMedia({
        video:{ width:{ ideal:1280 }, height:{ ideal:720 }, facingMode:'user' },
        audio:{ echoCancellation:true, noiseSuppression:true }
      });
    }catch(e){
      const denied = e && (e.name === 'NotAllowedError' || e.name === 'SecurityError');
      this.message(denied ? 'Camera permission was refused' : 'No camera could be opened',
        denied ? 'Allow it in your browser’s site settings, or upload a video instead.'
               : 'Nothing is plugged in, or another app is already using it. You can upload a video instead.');
      $('stHint').textContent = 'Use “Upload a video instead”.';
      return false;
    }

    const v = $('stVideo');
    v.srcObject = this.stream; v.muted = true; v.classList.add('mirror');
    try{ await v.play(); }catch(e){}
    $('stMsg').style.display = 'none';
    $('stHint').textContent = 'Looking good. Press the button again to start recording.';
    this.mode = 'live';
    return true;
  },

  /* A three-second run-in, because starting to talk the instant a camera
     turns on is how you get twenty clips that begin with "um". */
  countdown(){
    if (this.counting) return;
    this.counting = true;
    let n = 3;
    this.message(String(n), 'Get ready');
    const step = setInterval(() => {
      n--;
      if (n > 0){ this.message(String(n), 'Get ready'); return; }
      clearInterval(step);
      this.counting = false;
      $('stMsg').style.display = 'none';
      this.record();
    }, 800);
  },

  record(){
    const type = this.mime();
    if (!window.MediaRecorder || !type){
      this.message('This browser cannot record video',
        'MediaRecorder is not available. You can still upload a video you made elsewhere.');
      return;
    }

    this.chunks = [];
    try{ this.rec = new MediaRecorder(this.stream, { mimeType:type, videoBitsPerSecond:2_500_000 }); }
    catch(e){ this.rec = new MediaRecorder(this.stream); }

    this.rec.ondataavailable = e => { if (e.data && e.data.size) this.chunks.push(e.data); };
    this.rec.onstop = () => this.review(new Blob(this.chunks, { type:this.rec.mimeType || type }),
                                        (Date.now() - this.t0) / 1000);

    this.rec.start(250);
    this.t0 = Date.now();
    this.mode = 'rec';

    $('stDot').classList.add('live');
    $('stDot').setAttribute('aria-label', 'Stop recording');
    $('stRec').classList.add('on');
    $('stHint').textContent = 'Recording. Press the square to stop.';

    clearInterval(this.tick);
    this.tick = setInterval(() => {
      const ms = Date.now() - this.t0;
      $('stTime').textContent = clock(ms / 1000);
      if (ms >= this.MAX_MS){ toast('Ten minutes is the limit — saved what you had.'); this.stop(); }
    }, 250);
  },

  stop(){
    clearInterval(this.tick);
    if (this.rec && this.rec.state !== 'inactive') this.rec.stop();
  },

  /* Shows the clip back, whether it came from the camera or from a file. */
  review(blob, seconds){
    this.stopStream();
    this.blob = blob;
    this.dur = seconds;

    if (this.url) URL.revokeObjectURL(this.url);
    this.url = URL.createObjectURL(blob);

    const v = $('stVideo');
    v.srcObject = null;
    v.src = this.url;
    v.muted = false; v.controls = true; v.classList.remove('mirror');

    $('stMsg').style.display = 'none';
    $('stRec').classList.remove('on');
    $('stDot').classList.remove('live');
    $('stDot').disabled = true;
    $('stSave').style.display = '';
    $('stHint').textContent = 'Watch it back. Keep it, or go again.';
    this.mode = 'review';

    // A recorded webm often has no duration in its header, so the timer we
    // kept while recording is the honest number. An uploaded file does know.
    if (!seconds){
      v.onloadedmetadata = () => {
        if (isFinite(v.duration) && v.duration > 0) this.dur = v.duration;
      };
    }
  },

  fillTopics(){
    const sel = $('stTopic');
    const groups = {};
    for (const t of TOPICS) (groups[t.c] = groups[t.c] || []).push(t);

    sel.innerHTML = Object.keys(groups).sort().map(c =>
      `<optgroup label="${esc(c)}">${groups[c].map(t =>
        `<option value="${esc(t.t)}">${esc(t.t)}</option>`).join('')}</optgroup>`).join('');

    if (this.topic) sel.value = this.topic.t;
  },

  async save(){
    if (!this.blob) return;
    const title = $('stTopic').value;
    const t = TOPICS.find(x => x.t === title);

    const id = await Vault.add({
      topic: title,
      cat:   t ? t.c : '',
      at:    Date.now(),
      dur:   this.dur || 0,
      size:  this.blob.size,
      type:  this.blob.type || 'video/webm',
      blob:  this.blob
    });

    if (id === null){
      toast('This browser will not let the site store video.');
      return;
    }

    // Explaining it is a stronger signal of having learned it than a checkbox,
    // so saving a clip ticks the topic off if it was not already.
    if (t && !Progress.has(t)) Progress.toggle(t);

    this.close();
    toast('Saved to your gallery.');
    if (state.view === 'gallery') renderGallery();
    if (state.view === 'topic' && state.open) fillMine(state.open);
  }
};

/* ---- studio wiring ------------------------------------------------------ */
$('stClose').onclick = () => Studio.close();
$('studio').onclick = e => { if (e.target === $('studio')) Studio.close(); };

$('stDot').onclick = async () => {
  if (Studio.mode === 'off'){ if (await Studio.camera()) return; return; }
  if (Studio.mode === 'live') return Studio.countdown();
  if (Studio.mode === 'rec')  return Studio.stop();
};

$('stRetake').onclick = async () => { Studio.reset(); if (await Studio.camera()) {} };
$('stDrop').onclick   = () => Studio.close();
$('stKeep').onclick   = () => Studio.save();

$('stFile').onchange = e => {
  const f = e.target.files && e.target.files[0];
  e.target.value = '';
  if (!f) return;
  if (!f.type.startsWith('video/')) return toast('That is not a video file.');
  if (f.size > 300 * 1024 * 1024)   return toast('That file is too big to store in a browser.');
  Studio.review(f, 0);
};

/* =========================================================================
   THE GALLERY
   ========================================================================= */
let clipUrls = [];
function dropUrls(){ clipUrls.forEach(URL.revokeObjectURL); clipUrls = []; }

async function renderGallery(){
  dropUrls();
  const list = await Vault.all();

  $('galEmpty').style.display = list.length ? 'none' : '';
  $('clips').style.display    = list.length ? '' : 'none';

  const bytes = list.reduce((a, c) => a + (c.size || 0), 0);
  $('galInfo').textContent = list.length
    ? `${list.length} recording${list.length === 1 ? '' : 's'} · ${weight(bytes)} · stored in this browser`
    : '';

  $('clips').innerHTML = list.map(c => {
    const url = URL.createObjectURL(c.blob);
    clipUrls.push(url);
    const known = TOPICS.some(t => t.t === c.topic);
    return `<div class="clip">
      <video src="${url}" controls preload="metadata" playsinline></video>
      <div class="body">
        <h3>${esc(c.topic || 'Untitled')}</h3>
        <div class="sub">
          ${c.cat ? `<span class="chip cat">${esc(c.cat)}</span>` : ''}
          <span>${when(c.at)}</span>${c.dur ? `<span>· ${clock(c.dur)}</span>` : ''}
          <span>· ${weight(c.size || 0)}</span>
        </div>
        <div class="acts">
          ${known ? `<button data-open="${esc(c.topic)}">Re-read it</button>` : ''}
          <a href="${url}" download="${esc((c.topic || 'explanation').replace(/[^\w\- ]+/g, '')).slice(0, 60)}.${(c.type || '').includes('mp4') ? 'mp4' : 'webm'}">Download</a>
          <button class="danger" data-del="${c.id}">Delete</button>
        </div>
      </div>
    </div>`;
  }).join('');

  $('clips').querySelectorAll('[data-open]').forEach(b => {
    b.onclick = () => { const t = TOPICS.find(x => x.t === b.dataset.open); if (t) openTopic(t); };
  });

  // Two-step delete rather than a confirm box: the first press arms it, the
  // second does it, and clicking anywhere else disarms.
  $('clips').querySelectorAll('[data-del]').forEach(b => {
    b.onclick = async () => {
      if (!b.classList.contains('arm')){
        $('clips').querySelectorAll('.arm').forEach(o => { o.classList.remove('arm'); o.textContent = 'Delete'; });
        b.classList.add('arm'); b.textContent = 'Delete for good?';
        setTimeout(() => { b.classList.remove('arm'); b.textContent = 'Delete'; }, 4000);
        return;
      }
      await Vault.del(+b.dataset.del);
      renderGallery();
      toast('Deleted.');
    };
  });
}

$('galRecord').onclick     = () => Studio.open(null);
$('galEmptyRec').onclick   = () => Studio.open(null);
$('galEmptyBrowse').onclick = () => go('explore');

/* Recordings you already made for the topic you are reading. Filled in after
   the article renders, because reading them is a trip to IndexedDB. */
async function fillMine(t){
  const box = $('mine');
  if (!box) return;
  const list = (await Vault.all()).filter(c => c.topic === t.t);
  if (!list.length){ box.closest('.sec').style.display = 'none'; return; }

  box.closest('.sec').style.display = '';
  box.innerHTML = list.map(c => {
    const url = URL.createObjectURL(c.blob);
    clipUrls.push(url);
    return `<div><video src="${url}" controls preload="metadata" playsinline></video>
      <div class="sub" style="color:var(--ink-3);font-size:12.5px;margin-top:6px">
        ${when(c.at)}${c.dur ? ' · ' + clock(c.dur) : ''}</div></div>`;
  }).join('');
}

/* ---- chrome ------------------------------------------------------------- */
addEventListener('scroll', () => $('nav').classList.toggle('scrolled', scrollY > 4), { passive:true });

/* ---- boot --------------------------------------------------------------- */
$('q').placeholder = 'Search ' + TOPICS.length + ' topics…';
$('topicEl').innerHTML = idleMarkup();
$('topicEl').onclick = () => { if (!state.started) start(); else openTopic(current()); };
paintProgress();
renderStats();
</script>
</body></html>
